// ARQUIVO: lib/core/arch_constants.dart
class ArchConstants {
  // Animation
  static const Duration animationShort = Duration(milliseconds: 200);
  static const Duration animationMedium = Duration(milliseconds: 350);
  static const Duration animationLong = Duration(milliseconds: 500);
  
  // Sizes
  static const double appBarHeight = 80.0;
  static const double bottomNavHeight = 80.0;
  static const double maxContentWidth = 500.0;
  
  // API
  static const Duration apiTimeout = Duration(seconds: 30);
  static const int maxRetryAttempts = 3;
}
2. REFATORAÇÃO COMPLETA DO PAINEL PRINCIPAL
dart
// ARQUIVO: lib/ui/dashboard/arch_dashboard_screen.dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../core/arch_theme.dart';
import '../../core/arch_components.dart';

class ArchDashboardScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final String customerName;
  final String planName;
  final String connectionStatus;
  final double billAmount;
  final DateTime billDueDate;
  final double usedGb;
  final double totalGb;
  final double downloadMbps;
  final double uploadMbps;
  final Function(String) onNavigate;

  const ArchDashboardScreen({
    super.key,
    required this.customerName,
    required this.planName,
    required this.connectionStatus,
    required this.billAmount,
    required this.billDueDate,
    required this.usedGb,
    required this.totalGb,
    required this.downloadMbps,
    required this.uploadMbps,
    required this.onNavigate,
  });

  @override
  State<ArchDashboardScreen> createState() => _ArchDashboardScreenState();
}

class _ArchDashboardScreenState extends State<ArchDashboardScreen> 
    with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  
  late AnimationController _animationController;
  late Animation<double> _contentAnimation;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _animationController = AnimationController(
      vsync: this,
      duration: ArchConstants.animationLong,
    );
    
    _contentAnimation = CurvedAnimation(
      parent: _animationController,
      curve: Curves.easeOutCubic,
    );
    
    _animationController.forward();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _animationController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: ArchColors.background,
      body: CustomScrollView(
        physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(),
        slivers: [
          // Modern App Bar
          _buildAppBar(),
          
          // Dashboard Content
          SliverToBoxAdapter(
            child: SizeTransition(
              sizeFactor: _contentAnimation,
              child: Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(ArchSpacing.md),
                child: Column(
                  children: [
                    // Welcome Card
                    _buildWelcomeCard(),
                    const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.lg),
                    
                    // Status Grid
                    _buildStatusGrid(),
                    const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.xl),
                    
                    // Quick Actions
                    _buildQuickActions(),
                    const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.xl),
                    
                    // Usage Section
                    _buildUsageSection(),
                    const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.xl),
                    
                    // Support Card
                    _buildSupportCard(),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  SliverAppBar _buildAppBar() {
    return SliverAppBar(
      expandedHeight: 120,
      collapsedHeight: 80,
      backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
      elevation: 0,
      flexibleSpace: Container(
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          gradient: LinearGradient(
            colors: [
              ArchColors.surface.withOpacity(0.8),
              ArchColors.background.withOpacity(0.9),
            ],
            begin: Alignment.topCenter,
            end: Alignment.bottomCenter,
          ),
        ),
        child: FlexibleSpaceBar(
          title: Row(
            children: [
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  gradient: ArchColors.primaryGradient,
                  borderRadius: ArchBorders.circle,
                ),
                child: const Icon(Icons.network_wifi_rounded, 
                    size: 20, color: Colors.white),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: ArchSpacing.sm),
              Text(
                'Arch Telecom',
                style: ArchTextStyles.h3.copyWith(fontSize: 20),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          centerTitle: false,
          titlePadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: ArchSpacing.lg),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildWelcomeCard() {
    return ArchCard(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(ArchSpacing.lg),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          // Avatar
          Container(
            width: 60,
            height: 60,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              gradient: ArchColors.primaryGradient,
              shape: BoxShape.circle,
            ),
            child: Icon(Icons.person_rounded, 
                size: 30, color: Colors.white),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: ArchSpacing.lg),
          
          // User Info
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(
                  'Olá, ${widget.customerName.split(' ').first}!',
                  style: ArchTextStyles.h3,
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.xs),
                Text(
                  widget.planName,
                  style: ArchTextStyles.bodySmall.copyWith(
                    color: ArchColors.textSecondary,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.sm),
                _buildConnectionStatus(),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildConnectionStatus() {
    final isOnline = widget.connectionStatus.toLowerCase() == 'ativo';
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 6),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: isOnline 
            ? ArchColors.success.withOpacity(0.1)
            : ArchColors.error.withOpacity(0.1),
        borderRadius: ArchBorders.small,
        border: Border.all(
          color: isOnline 
              ? ArchColors.success.withOpacity(0.3)
              : ArchColors.error.withOpacity(0.3),
        ),
      ),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          Container(
            width: 6,
            height: 6,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: isOnline ? ArchColors.success : ArchColors.error,
              shape: BoxShape.circle,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 6),
          Text(
            isOnline ? 'Online' : 'Offline',
            style: ArchTextStyles.caption.copyWith(
              color: isOnline ? ArchColors.success : ArchColors.error,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildStatusGrid() {
    return GridView.count(
      shrinkWrap: true,
      physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
      crossAxisCount: 2,
      crossAxisSpacing: ArchSpacing.md,
      mainAxisSpacing: ArchSpacing.md,
      childAspectRatio: 1.1,
      children: [
        _buildMetricCard(
          title: 'Download',
          value: '${widget.downloadMbps.toStringAsFixed(0)} Mbps',
          subtitle: 'Velocidade atual',
          icon: Icons.download_rounded,
          color: ArchColors.secondary,
          trend: '+5%',
        ),
        _buildMetricCard(
          title: 'Upload',
          value: '${widget.uploadMbps.toStringAsFixed(0)} Mbps',
          subtitle: 'Velocidade atual',
          icon: Icons.upload_rounded,
          color: ArchColors.accent,
          trend: '+2%',
        ),
        _buildMetricCard(
          title: 'Ping',
          value: '12 ms',
          subtitle: 'Latência',
          icon: Icons.speed_rounded,
          color: ArchColors.info,
          trend: '-3%',
        ),
        _buildMetricCard(
          title: 'Fatura',
          value: 'R\$${widget.billAmount.toStringAsFixed(2)}',
          subtitle: _getDueDateText(),
          icon: Icons.receipt_rounded,
          color: ArchColors.warning,
          trend: _getDueDateTrend(),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildMetricCard({
    required String title,
    required String value,
    required String subtitle,
    required IconData icon,
    required Color color,
    required String trend,
  }) {
    return ArchCard(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(ArchSpacing.lg),
      onTap: () {}, // Add specific navigation
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: color.withOpacity(0.1),
                  borderRadius: ArchBorders.medium,
                ),
                child: Icon(icon, size: 20, color: color),
              ),
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: trend.startsWith('+') 
                      ? ArchColors.success.withOpacity(0.1)
                      : ArchColors.error.withOpacity(0.1),
                  borderRadius: ArchBorders.small,
                ),
                child: Text(
                  trend,
                  style: ArchTextStyles.caption.copyWith(
                    color: trend.startsWith('+') 
                        ? ArchColors.success 
                        : ArchColors.error,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.md),
          Text(title, style: ArchTextStyles.bodySmall),
          const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.xs),
          Text(value, style: ArchTextStyles.h3),
          const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.xs),
          Text(subtitle, 
            style: ArchTextStyles.caption.copyWith(
              color: ArchColors.textTertiary,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildQuickActions() {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Text('Ações Rápidas', style: ArchTextStyles.h3),
        const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.md),
        GridView.count(
          shrinkWrap: true,
          physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
          crossAxisCount: 4,
          crossAxisSpacing: ArchSpacing.md,
          mainAxisSpacing: ArchSpacing.md,
          children: [
            _QuickActionTile(
              icon: Icons.speed_rounded,
              label: 'Teste Veloc.',
              color: ArchColors.secondary,
              onTap: () => widget.onNavigate('speed_test'),
            ),
            _QuickActionTile(
              icon: Icons.receipt_long_rounded,
              label: 'Faturas',
              color: ArchColors.warning,
              onTap: () => widget.onNavigate('invoices'),
            ),
            _QuickActionTile(
              icon: Icons.support_agent_rounded,
              label: 'Suporte',
              color: ArchColors.info,
              onTap: () => widget.onNavigate('support'),
            ),
            _QuickActionTile(
              icon: Icons.network_check_rounded,
              label: 'Diagnóstico',
              color: ArchColors.accent,
              onTap: () => widget.onNavigate('network_diagnostic'),
            ),
            _QuickActionTile(
              icon: Icons.data_usage_rounded,
              label: 'Consumo',
              color: ArchColors.success,
              onTap: () => widget.onNavigate('internet_usage'),
            ),
            _QuickActionTile(
              icon: Icons.qr_code_rounded,
              label: 'Meu IP',
              color: ArchColors.primary,
              onTap: () => widget.onNavigate('my_ip'),
            ),
            _QuickActionTile(
              icon: Icons.payment_rounded,
              label: 'Pagamento',
              color: ArchColors.error,
              onTap: () => widget.onNavigate('payment_promise'),
            ),
            _QuickActionTile(
              icon: Icons.lightbulb_rounded,
              label: 'Dicas',
              color: ArchColors.warning,
              onTap: () => widget.onNavigate('useful_tips'),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildUsageSection() {
    final double usagePercent = widget.totalGb > 0 
        ? (widget.usedGb / widget.totalGb).clamp(0.0, 1.0) 
        : 0.0;
    
    return ArchCard(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(ArchSpacing.lg),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Text('Consumo de Internet', style: ArchTextStyles.h3),
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 6),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: _getUsageColor(usagePercent).withOpacity(0.1),
                  borderRadius: ArchBorders.small,
                ),
                child: Text(
                  '${(usagePercent * 100).toStringAsFixed(0)}%',
                  style: ArchTextStyles.button.copyWith(
                    color: _getUsageColor(usagePercent),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.lg),
          
          // Usage Progress
          _UsageProgressBar(
            usedGb: widget.usedGb,
            totalGb: widget.totalGb,
            percentage: usagePercent,
          ),
          
          const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.lg),
          ArchButton(
            text: 'Ver Consumo Detalhado',
            onPressed: () => widget.onNavigate('internet_usage'),
            filled: false,
            width: double.infinity,
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSupportCard() {
    return ArchCard(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(ArchSpacing.lg),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: ArchColors.accent.withOpacity(0.1),
              borderRadius: ArchBorders.medium,
            ),
            child: Icon(Icons.support_rounded, 
                size: 24, color: ArchColors.accent),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: ArchSpacing.lg),
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text('Precisa de ajuda?', style: ArchTextStyles.h3),
                const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.xs),
                Text('Nossa equipe está disponível 24/7',
                    style: ArchTextStyles.bodySmall),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          IconButton(
            icon: Icon(Icons.arrow_forward_rounded, 
                color: ArchColors.primary),
            onPressed: () => widget.onNavigate('support'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Color _getUsageColor(double percent) {
    if (percent < 0.6) return ArchColors.success;
    if (percent < 0.9) return ArchColors.warning;
    return ArchColors.error;
  }

  String _getDueDateText() {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final difference = widget.billDueDate.difference(now);
    final days = difference.inDays;
    
    if (days == 0) return 'Vence hoje';
    if (days == 1) return 'Vence amanhã';
    if (days > 1) return 'Vence em $days dias';
    return 'Vencida há ${days.abs()} dias';
  }

  String _getDueDateTrend() {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final difference = widget.billDueDate.difference(now);
    return difference.inDays >= 0 ? '!' : '⚠️';
  }
}

class _QuickActionTile extends StatelessWidget {
  final IconData icon;
  final String label;
  final Color color;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  const _QuickActionTile({
    required this.icon,
    required this.label,
    required this.color,
    required this.onTap,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Material(
      color: Colors.transparent,
      borderRadius: ArchBorders.medium,
      child: InkWell(
        onTap: onTap,
        borderRadius: ArchBorders.medium,
        child: Container(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(ArchSpacing.md),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            borderRadius: ArchBorders.medium,
            border: Border.all(
              color: color.withOpacity(0.2),
              width: 1,
            ),
          ),
          child: Column(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
            children: [
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: color.withOpacity(0.1),
                  borderRadius: ArchBorders.small,
                ),
                child: Icon(icon, size: 20, color: color),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              Text(
                label,
                textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                style: ArchTextStyles.caption.copyWith(
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                ),
                maxLines: 2,
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _UsageProgressBar extends StatelessWidget {
  final double usedGb;
  final double totalGb;
  final double percentage;

  const _UsageProgressBar({
    required this.usedGb,
    required this.totalGb,
    required this.percentage,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Column(
      children: [
        // Progress Bar
        Container(
          height: 12,
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: ArchColors.surface,
            borderRadius: ArchBorders.circle,
          ),
          child: Stack(
            children: [
              LayoutBuilder(
                builder: (context, constraints) {
                  return AnimatedContainer(
                    duration: ArchConstants.animationMedium,
                    curve: Curves.easeOutCubic,
                    width: constraints.maxWidth * percentage,
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      gradient: ArchColors.primaryGradient,
                      borderRadius: ArchBorders.circle,
                    ),
                  );
                },
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.md),
        
        // Usage Info
        Row(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
          children: [
            Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text('Usado', style: ArchTextStyles.bodySmall),
                Text('${usedGb.toStringAsFixed(1)} GB', 
                  style: ArchTextStyles.body.copyWith(
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
              children: [
                Text('Disponível', style: ArchTextStyles.bodySmall),
                Text('${(totalGb - usedGb).toStringAsFixed(1)} GB', 
                  style: ArchTextStyles.body.copyWith(
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}
3. NOVA TELA DE CONSUMO MODERNA
dart
// ARQUIVO: lib/ui/consumption/arch_consumption_screen.dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';
import '../../core/arch_theme.dart';
import '../../core/arch_components.dart';
import '../../providers/consumo_provider.dart';

class ArchConsumptionScreen extends StatelessWidget {
  final String cpfCnpj;
  final String senha;

  const ArchConsumptionScreen({
    super.key,
    required this.cpfCnpj,
    required this.senha,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: ArchColors.background,
      appBar: ArchAppBar(
        title: 'Meu Consumo',
        showBackButton: true,
      ),
      body: Consumer<ConsumoProvider>(
        builder: (context, provider, child) {
          switch (provider.state) {
            case ConsumoState.loading:
              return _buildLoadingState();
            case ConsumoState.error:
              return _buildErrorState(context, provider.errorMessage!);
            case ConsumoState.success:
              final data = provider.consumptionData ?? {};
              return _buildContent(data);
            default:
              return _buildLoadingState();
          }
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildContent(Map<String, dynamic> data) {
    final double usedGb = (data['usedGb'] as num? ?? 0).toDouble();
    final double totalGb = (data['totalGb'] as num? ?? 1000).toDouble();
    final String planName = data['planName'] as String? ?? 'Plano não informado';
    final String period = data['period'] as String? ?? 'N/A';
    final double percentage = totalGb > 0 ? (usedGb / totalGb).clamp(0.0, 1.0) : 0.0;

    return SingleChildScrollView(
      physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(ArchSpacing.md),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          // Header Card
          _buildHeaderCard(period, planName),
          const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.xl),
          
          // Usage Ring
          _buildUsageRing(usedGb, totalGb, percentage),
          const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.xl),
          
          // Usage Details
          _buildUsageDetails(usedGb, totalGb, percentage),
          const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.xl),
          
          // History Chart
          _buildHistoryChart(),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildHeaderCard(String period, String planName) {
    return ArchCard(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(ArchSpacing.lg),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          Text('Ciclo Atual', style: ArchTextStyles.bodySmall),
          const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.xs),
          Text(period, style: ArchTextStyles.h2),
          const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.lg),
          Divider(color: ArchColors.textTertiary.withOpacity(0.3)),
          const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.lg),
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Text('PLANO ATUAL', 
                    style: ArchTextStyles.caption.copyWith(
                      color: ArchColors.primary,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.xs),
                  Text(planName, style: ArchTextStyles.h3),
                ],
              ),
              Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
                children: [
                  Text('FRANQUIA', 
                    style: ArchTextStyles.caption.copyWith(
                      color: ArchColors.textTertiary,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.xs),
                  Text('${totalGb.toStringAsFixed(0)} GB', 
                    style: ArchTextStyles.h3.copyWith(
                      color: ArchColors.textPrimary,
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildUsageRing(double usedGb, double totalGb, double percentage) {
    return Container(
      width: 220,
      height: 220,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        shape: BoxShape.circle,
        boxShadow: ArchShadows.large,
      ),
      child: Stack(
        alignment: Alignment.center,
        children: [
          // Background Circle
          Container(
            width: 200,
            height: 200,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              shape: BoxShape.circle,
              color: ArchColors.surface,
            ),
          ),
          
          // Progress Circle
          SizedBox(
            width: 180,
            height: 180,
            child: CircularProgressIndicator(
              value: percentage,
              strokeWidth: 8,
              backgroundColor: ArchColors.textTertiary.withOpacity(0.2),
              valueColor: AlwaysStoppedAnimation<Color>(
                _getUsageColor(percentage),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          
          // Content
          Column(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
            children: [
              Text(
                usedGb.toStringAsFixed(1),
                style: ArchTextStyles.h1.copyWith(fontSize: 36),
              ),
              Text(
                'GB USADOS',
                style: ArchTextStyles.bodySmall.copyWith(
                  color: ArchColors.textSecondary,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildUsageDetails(double usedGb, double totalGb, double percentage) {
    return ArchCard(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(ArchSpacing.lg),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              _UsageDetailItem(
                label: 'Usado',
                value: '${usedGb.toStringAsFixed(1)} GB',
                color: _getUsageColor(percentage),
              ),
              _UsageDetailItem(
                label: 'Disponível',
                value: '${(totalGb - usedGb).toStringAsFixed(1)} GB',
                color: ArchColors.success,
              ),
              _UsageDetailItem(
                label: 'Total',
                value: '${totalGb.toStringAsFixed(0)} GB',
                color: ArchColors.textTertiary,
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.lg),
          LinearProgressIndicator(
            value: percentage,
            backgroundColor: ArchColors.textTertiary.withOpacity(0.2),
            valueColor: AlwaysStoppedAnimation<Color>(
              _getUsageColor(percentage),
            ),
            borderRadius: ArchBorders.circle,
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildHistoryChart() {
    // Placeholder for chart - would integrate with fl_chart
    return ArchCard(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(ArchSpacing.lg),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Text('Histórico de Consumo', style: ArchTextStyles.h3),
          const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.lg),
          Container(
            height: 200,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              borderRadius: ArchBorders.medium,
              color: ArchColors.surface,
            ),
            child: Center(
              child: Text(
                'Gráfico de Consumo\n(Em desenvolvimento)',
                textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                style: ArchTextStyles.body.copyWith(
                  color: ArchColors.textTertiary,
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildLoadingState() {
    return Center(
      child: Column(
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
        children: [
          SizedBox(
            width: 80,
            height: 80,
            child: CircularProgressIndicator(
              strokeWidth: 3,
              valueColor: AlwaysStoppedAnimation(ArchColors.primary),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.lg),
          Text('Carregando consumo...', style: ArchTextStyles.body),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildErrorState(BuildContext context, String error) {
    return Center(
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(ArchSpacing.xl),
        child: Column(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
          children: [
            Icon(
              Icons.error_outline_rounded,
              size: 64,
              color: ArchColors.error,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.lg),
            Text(
              'Erro ao Carregar',
              style: ArchTextStyles.h2,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.md),
            Text(
              error,
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
              style: ArchTextStyles.body.copyWith(
                color: ArchColors.textSecondary,
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.xl),
            ArchButton(
              text: 'Tentar Novamente',
              onPressed: () {
                context.read<ConsumoProvider>().fetchConsumptionData();
              },
              width: 200,
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Color _getUsageColor(double percent) {
    if (percent < 0.6) return ArchColors.success;
    if (percent < 0.9) return ArchColors.warning;
    return ArchColors.error;
  }
}

class _UsageDetailItem extends StatelessWidget {
  final String label;
  final String value;
  final Color color;

  const _UsageDetailItem({
    required this.label,
    required this.value,
    required this.color,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Column(
      children: [
        Text(label, style: ArchTextStyles.caption),
        const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.xs),
        Text(value, 
          style: ArchTextStyles.body.copyWith(
            color: color,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

// Custom App Bar Component
class ArchAppBar extends StatelessWidget implements PreferredSizeWidget {
  final String title;
  final bool showBackButton;
  final List<Widget>? actions;

  const ArchAppBar({
    super.key,
    required this.title,
    this.showBackButton = false,
    this.actions,
  });

  @override
  Size get preferredSize => const Size.fromHeight(80);

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return AppBar(
      backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
      elevation: 0,
      leading: showBackButton 
          ? IconButton(
              icon: Icon(Icons.arrow_back_rounded, color: ArchColors.textPrimary),
              onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
            )
          : null,
      title: Text(title, style: ArchTextStyles.h2.copyWith(fontSize: 24)),
      actions: actions,
    );
  }
}
4. ATUALIZAÇÃO DO MAIN.DART COM NOVO SISTEMA
dart
// ATUALIZAÇÃO NO main.dart
class MyApp extends StatefulWidget {
  const MyApp({super.key});

  @override
  State<MyApp> createState() => _MyAppState();
}

class _MyAppState extends State<MyApp> {
  late final Future<void> _initialization;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _initialization = _initializeApp();
  }

  Future<void> _initializeApp() async {
    // Initialize Arch Design System
    await ArchDesignSystem.initialize();
    
    LocalNotificationService.initialize();
    await FirebaseMessaging.instance.requestPermission();
    
    FirebaseMessaging.onMessage.listen((RemoteMessage message) {
      print('✅ Mensagem recebida em primeiro plano!');
      LocalNotificationService.showNotification(message);
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return MaterialApp(
      debugShowCheckedModeBanner: false,
      title: 'Arch Telecom',
      theme: ArchTheme.darkTheme,
      home: FutureBuilder(
        future: _initialization,
        builder: (context, snapshot) {
          if (snapshot.hasError) {
            return _buildErrorScreen(snapshot.error.toString());
          }
          if (snapshot.connectionState == ConnectionState.done) {
            return const ArchAuthGate();
          }
          return const ArchSplashScreen();
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildErrorScreen(String error) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: ArchColors.background,
      body: Center(
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(ArchSpacing.xl),
          child: Column(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
            children: [
              Icon(
                Icons.error_outline_rounded,
                size: 80,
                color: ArchColors.error,
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.xl),
              Text(
                'Erro de Inicialização',
                style: ArchTextStyles.h1,
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.lg),
              Text(
                error,
                textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                style: ArchTextStyles.body.copyWith(
                  color: ArchColors.textSecondary,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class ArchAuthGate extends StatefulWidget {
  const ArchAuthGate({super.key});

  @override
  State<ArchAuthGate> createState() => _ArchAuthGateState();
}

class _ArchAuthGateState extends State<ArchAuthGate> {
  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      Provider.of<ConfigurationProvider>(context, listen: false)
          .loadConfig(providerId);
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Consumer2<ConfigurationProvider, AuthService>(
      builder: (context, configProvider, authService, child) {
        if (configProvider.isLoading || authService.isLoading) {
          return const ArchSplashScreen();
        }

        if (configProvider.errorMessage != null) {
          return _buildConfigErrorScreen(configProvider.errorMessage!);
        }

        if (authService.isAuthenticated) {
          return const ArchMainNavigator();
        } else {
          return const ArchLoginScreen();
        }
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _buildConfigErrorScreen(String error) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: ArchColors.background,
      body: Center(
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(ArchSpacing.xl),
          child: Column(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
            children: [
              Icon(
                Icons.cloud_off_rounded,
                size: 80,
                color: ArchColors.warning,
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.xl),
              Text(
                'Configuração Indisponível',
                style: ArchTextStyles.h1,
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.lg),
              Text(
                error,
                textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                style: ArchTextStyles.body.copyWith(
                  color: ArchColors.textSecondary,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.xl),
              ArchButton(
                text: 'Tentar Novamente',
                onPressed: () {
                  Provider.of<ConfigurationProvider>(context, listen: false)
                      .loadConfig(providerId);
                },
                width: 200,
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// New Main Navigator with Bottom Navigation
class ArchMainNavigator extends StatefulWidget {
  const ArchMainNavigator({super.key});

  @override
  State<ArchMainNavigator> createState() => _ArchMainNavigatorState();
}

class _ArchMainNavigatorState extends State<ArchMainNavigator> {
  int _currentIndex = 0;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: ArchColors.background,
      body: _getCurrentScreen(),
      bottomNavigationBar: ArchNavigationBar(
        currentIndex: _currentIndex,
        onTap: (index) => setState(() => _currentIndex = index),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _getCurrentScreen() {
    // This would be replaced with actual screen logic
    switch (_currentIndex) {
      case 0:
        return const ArchDashboardScreen(
          customerName: 'Cliente',
          planName: 'Plano Arch 500MB',
          connectionStatus: 'Ativo',
          billAmount: 99.90,
          billDueDate: DateTime.now().add(const Duration(days: 5)),
          usedGb: 87.5,
          totalGb: 300,
          downloadMbps: 480.3,
          uploadMbps: 245.8,
          onNavigate: (page) {},
        );
      case 1:
        return const ArchConsumptionScreen(
          cpfCnpj: '',
          senha: '',
        );
      default:
        return Container();
    }
  }
}

5. SISTEMA DE SUPORTE MODERNO
dart
// ARQUIVO: lib/ui/support/arch_support_screen.dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../core/arch_theme.dart';
import '../../core/arch_components.dart';

class ArchSupportScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final String cpfCnpj;
  final String clientName;

  const ArchSupportScreen({
    super.key,
    required this.cpfCnpj,
    required this.clientName,
  });

  @override
  State<ArchSupportScreen> createState() => _ArchSupportScreenState();
}

class _ArchSupportScreenState extends State<ArchSupportScreen> 
    with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  
  late TabController _tabController;
  int _selectedTab = 0;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _tabController = TabController(length: 3, vsync: this);
    _tabController.addListener(_handleTabSelection);
  }

  void _handleTabSelection() {
    if (_tabController.indexIsChanging) {
      setState(() => _selectedTab = _tabController.index);
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _tabController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: ArchColors.background,
      appBar: ArchAppBar(
        title: 'Suporte',
        showBackButton: true,
      ),
      body: Column(
        children: [
          // Tab Bar
          Container(
            margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: ArchSpacing.md),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: ArchColors.surface,
              borderRadius: ArchBorders.large,
            ),
            child: TabBar(
              controller: _tabController,
              indicator: BoxDecoration(
                gradient: ArchColors.primaryGradient,
                borderRadius: ArchBorders.medium,
              ),
              labelColor: Colors.white,
              unselectedLabelColor: ArchColors.textTertiary,
              labelStyle: ArchTextStyles.button,
              tabs: const [
                Tab(text: 'Ajuda'),
                Tab(text: 'Chamados'),
                Tab(text: 'Contato'),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.lg),
          
          // Tab Content
          Expanded(
            child: TabBarView(
              controller: _tabController,
              children: [
                _buildHelpTab(),
                _buildTicketsTab(),
                _buildContactTab(),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildHelpTab() {
    final helpItems = [
      _HelpItem(
        icon: Icons.wifi_tethering_error_rounded,
        title: 'Problemas de Conexão',
        description: 'Solução para lentidão ou falta de internet',

class _HelpItem extends StatelessWidget {
  final IconData icon;
  final String title;
  final String description;
  final Color color;

  const _HelpItem({
    required this.icon,
    required this.title,
    required this.description,
    required this.color,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Material(
      color: Colors.transparent,
      borderRadius: ArchBorders.medium,
      child: InkWell(
        onTap: () {}, // Navigate to solution
        borderRadius: ArchBorders.medium,
        child: Container(
          width: 160,
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(ArchSpacing.lg),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            border: Border.all(color: color.withOpacity(0.2)),
            borderRadius: ArchBorders.medium,
          ),
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: color.withOpacity(0.1),
                  borderRadius: ArchBorders.small,
                ),
                child: Icon(icon, size: 20, color: color),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.md),
              Text(title, style: ArchTextStyles.body.copyWith(
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
              )),
              const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.xs),
              Text(description,
                style: ArchTextStyles.caption,
                maxLines: 2,
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _FAQItem extends StatefulWidget {
  final String question;
  final String answer;

  const _FAQItem({
    required this.question,
    required this.answer,
  });

  @override
  State<_FAQItem> createState() => _FAQItemState();
}

class _FAQItemState extends State<_FAQItem> {
  bool _isExpanded = false;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: ArchSpacing.md),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        border: Border.all(color: ArchColors.textTertiary.withOpacity(0.2)),
        borderRadius: ArchBorders.medium,
      ),
      child: Material(
        color: Colors.transparent,
        child: InkWell(
          onTap: () => setState(() => _isExpanded = !_isExpanded),
          borderRadius: ArchBorders.medium,
          child: Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(ArchSpacing.lg),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Row(
                  children: [
                    Expanded(
                      child: Text(
                        widget.question,
                        style: ArchTextStyles.body.copyWith(
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    Icon(
                      _isExpanded 
                          ? Icons.expand_less_rounded 
                          : Icons.expand_more_rounded,
                      color: ArchColors.textTertiary,
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                if (_isExpanded) ...[
                  const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.md),
                  Text(
                    widget.answer,
                    style: ArchTextStyles.bodySmall.copyWith(
                      color: ArchColors.textSecondary,
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _TicketItem extends StatelessWidget {
  final String id;
  final String subject;
  final String status;
  final Color statusColor;
  final String date;
  final String description;

  const _TicketItem({
    required this.id,
    required this.subject,
    required this.status,
    required this.statusColor,
    required this.date,
    required this.description,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: ArchSpacing.md),
      child: ArchCard(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(ArchSpacing.lg),
        onTap: () {}, // View ticket details
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Row(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
              children: [
                Text(id, style: ArchTextStyles.caption.copyWith(
                  color: ArchColors.primary,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                )),
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: statusColor.withOpacity(0.1),
                    borderRadius: ArchBorders.small,
                  ),
                  child: Text(
                    status,
                    style: ArchTextStyles.caption.copyWith(
                      color: statusColor,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.sm),
            Text(subject, style: ArchTextStyles.h3.copyWith(fontSize: 18)),
            const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.xs),
            Text(description, 
              style: ArchTextStyles.bodySmall.copyWith(
                color: ArchColors.textSecondary,
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.sm),
            Text(date, 
              style: ArchTextStyles.caption.copyWith(
                color: ArchColors.textTertiary,
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _ContactMethod extends StatelessWidget {
  final IconData icon;
  final String title;
  final String subtitle;
  final Color color;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  const _ContactMethod({
    required this.icon,
    required this.title,
    required this.subtitle,
    required this.color,
    required this.onTap,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: ArchSpacing.md),
      child: Material(
        color: Colors.transparent,
        borderRadius: ArchBorders.medium,
        child: InkWell(
          onTap: onTap,
          borderRadius: ArchBorders.medium,
          child: Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(ArchSpacing.lg),
            child: Row(
              children: [
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: color.withOpacity(0.1),
                    borderRadius: ArchBorders.medium,
                  ),
                  child: Icon(icon, size: 24, color: color),
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: ArchSpacing.lg),
                Expanded(
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Text(title, style: ArchTextStyles.body.copyWith(
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                      )),
                      Text(subtitle, 
                        style: ArchTextStyles.bodySmall.copyWith(
                          color: ArchColors.textSecondary,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                Icon(Icons.arrow_forward_rounded, 
                    color: ArchColors.textTertiary),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _ServiceHourItem extends StatelessWidget {
  final String day;
  final String hours;
  final bool isEmergency;

  const _ServiceHourItem({
    required this.day,
    required this.hours,
    this.isEmergency = false,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: ArchSpacing.md),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(ArchSpacing.lg),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: isEmergency 
            ? ArchColors.error.withOpacity(0.05)
            : ArchColors.surface,
        borderRadius: ArchBorders.medium,
        border: Border.all(
          color: isEmergency 
              ? ArchColors.error.withOpacity(0.2)
              : Colors.transparent,
        ),
      ),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
        children: [
          Text(day, style: ArchTextStyles.body.copyWith(
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
            color: isEmergency ? ArchColors.error : null,
          )),
          Text(hours, style: ArchTextStyles.body.copyWith(
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
            color: isEmergency ? ArchColors.error : ArchColors.primary,
          )),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
6. SISTEMA FINANCEIRO AVANÇADO
dart
// ARQUIVO: lib/ui/finance/arch_finance_screen.dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:intl/intl.dart';
import 'package:fl_chart/fl_chart.dart';
import '../../core/arch_theme.dart';
import '../../core/arch_components.dart';

class ArchFinanceScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final String cpfCnpj;
  final String senha;

  const ArchFinanceScreen({
    super.key,
    required this.cpfCnpj,
    required this.senha,
  });

  @override
  State<ArchFinanceScreen> createState() => _ArchFinanceScreenState();
}

class _ArchFinanceScreenState extends State<ArchFinanceScreen> 
    with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  
  late TabController _tabController;
  final List<Invoice> _invoices = [];
  final Map<int, double> _monthlyData = {};

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _tabController = TabController(length: 2, vsync: this);
    _loadMockData();
  }

  void _loadMockData() {
    // Mock invoices data
    _invoices.addAll([
      Invoice(
        number: '001234',
        amount: 99.90,
        dueDate: DateTime.now().subtract(const Duration(days: 5)),
        status: InvoiceStatus.overdue,
        paymentDate: null,
        barcode: '12345678901234567890123456789012345678901234',
        pixCode: '00020126580014br.gov.bcb.pix0136123e4567-e12b-12d1-a456-426614174000',
        pdfUrl: 'https://example.com/invoice.pdf',
      ),
      Invoice(
        number: '001233',
        amount: 99.90,
        dueDate: DateTime.now().add(const Duration(days: 10)),
        status: InvoiceStatus.pending,
        paymentDate: null,
        barcode: '12345678901234567890123456789012345678901234',
        pixCode: '00020126580014br.gov.bcb.pix0136123e4567-e12b-12d1-a456-426614174000',
        pdfUrl: 'https://example.com/invoice.pdf',
      ),
      Invoice(
        number: '001232',
        amount: 99.90,
        dueDate: DateTime.now().subtract(const Duration(days: 35)),
        status: InvoiceStatus.paid,
        paymentDate: DateTime.now().subtract(const Duration(days: 30)),
        barcode: '12345678901234567890123456789012345678901234',
        pixCode: '00020126580014br.gov.bcb.pix0136123e4567-e12b-12d1-a456-426614174000',
        pdfUrl: 'https://example.com/invoice.pdf',
      ),
    ]);

    // Mock monthly data
    final now = DateTime.now();
    for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
      _monthlyData[now.month - i] = (i + 1) * 99.90;
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _tabController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: ArchColors.background,
      appBar: ArchAppBar(
        title: 'Financeiro',
        showBackButton: true,
      ),
      body: Column(
        children: [
          // Summary Cards
          _buildSummaryCards(),
          const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.lg),
          
          // Tab Bar
          Container(
            margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: ArchSpacing.md),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: ArchColors.surface,
              borderRadius: ArchBorders.large,
            ),
            child: TabBar(
              controller: _tabController,
              indicator: BoxDecoration(
                gradient: ArchColors.primaryGradient,
                borderRadius: ArchBorders.medium,
              ),
              labelColor: Colors.white,
              unselectedLabelColor: ArchColors.textTertiary,
              labelStyle: ArchTextStyles.button,
              tabs: const [
                Tab(text: 'Faturas'),
                Tab(text: 'Histórico'),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.lg),
          
          // Tab Content
          Expanded(
            child: TabBarView(
              controller: _tabController,
              children: [
                _buildInvoicesTab(),
                _buildHistoryTab(),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSummaryCards() {
    final pendingInvoices = _invoices.where((i) => i.status == InvoiceStatus.pending).length;
    final overdueInvoices = _invoices.where((i) => i.status == InvoiceStatus.overdue).length;
    final totalPending = _invoices
        .where((i) => i.status != InvoiceStatus.paid)
        .fold(0.0, (sum, invoice) => sum + invoice.amount);

    return SingleChildScrollView(
      scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: ArchSpacing.md),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          _SummaryCard(
            title: 'Total Pendente',
            value: 'R\$${totalPending.toStringAsFixed(2)}',
            icon: Icons.receipt_long_rounded,
            color: ArchColors.warning,
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: ArchSpacing.md),
          _SummaryCard(
            title: 'Faturas Pendentes',
            value: pendingInvoices.toString(),
            icon: Icons.pending_actions_rounded,
            color: ArchColors.info,
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: ArchSpacing.md),
          _SummaryCard(
            title: 'Faturas Atrasadas',
            value: overdueInvoices.toString(),
            icon: Icons.warning_rounded,
            color: ArchColors.error,
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildInvoicesTab() {
    return Column(
      children: [
        // Quick Actions
        Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: ArchSpacing.md),
          child: Row(
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: ArchButton(
                  text: 'Pagar Fatura',
                  onPressed: _payInvoice,
                  icon: Icons.payment_rounded,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: ArchSpacing.md),
              Expanded(
                child: ArchButton(
                  text: '2ª Via',
                  onPressed: _downloadInvoice,
                  filled: false,
                  icon: Icons.download_rounded,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.lg),
        
        // Invoices List
        Expanded(
          child: ListView.builder(
            physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(),
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: ArchSpacing.md),
            itemCount: _invoices.length,
            itemBuilder: (context, index) => _InvoiceCard(
              invoice: _invoices[index],
              onTap: () => _showInvoiceDetails(_invoices[index]),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildHistoryTab() {
    return SingleChildScrollView(
      physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: ArchSpacing.md),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          // Spending Chart
          ArchCard(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(ArchSpacing.lg),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text('Histórico de Gastos', style: ArchTextStyles.h3),
                const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.lg),
                SizedBox(
                  height: 200,
                  child: BarChart(
                    BarChartData(
                      barTouchData: BarTouchData(enabled: false),
                      titlesData: FlTitlesData(
                        bottomTitles: AxisTitles(
                          sideTitles: SideTitles(
                            showTitles: true,
                            getTitlesWidget: (value, meta) {
                              final month = DateFormat('MMM').format(
                                DateTime(2024, value.toInt(), 1)
                              );
                              return Padding(
                                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 8.0),
                                child: Text(
                                  month,
                                  style: ArchTextStyles.caption,
                                ),
                              );
                            },
                          ),
                        ),
                        leftTitles: const AxisTitles(
                          sideTitles: SideTitles(showTitles: false),
                        ),
                        topTitles: const AxisTitles(
                          sideTitles: SideTitles(showTitles: false),
                        ),
                        rightTitles: const AxisTitles(
                          sideTitles: SideTitles(showTitles: false),
                        ),
                      ),
                      borderData: FlBorderData(show: false),
                      gridData: const FlGridData(show: false),
                      barGroups: _monthlyData.entries.map((entry) {
                        return BarChartGroupData(
                          x: entry.key,
                          barRods: [
                            BarChartRodData(
                              toY: entry.value,
                              color: ArchColors.primary,
                              width: 16,
                              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
                            ),
                          ],
                        );
                      }).toList(),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.lg),
          
          // Payment History
          ArchCard(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(ArchSpacing.lg),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text('Últimos Pagamentos', style: ArchTextStyles.h3),
                const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.lg),
                ..._invoices.where((i) => i.status == InvoiceStatus.paid).map(
                  (invoice) => _PaymentHistoryItem(invoice: invoice),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  void _payInvoice() {
    // Implement payment logic
  }

  void _downloadInvoice() {
    // Implement download logic
  }

  void _showInvoiceDetails(Invoice invoice) {
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      backgroundColor: ArchColors.surface,
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(24)),
      ),
      builder: (context) => _InvoiceDetailsModal(invoice: invoice),
    );
  }
}

class _SummaryCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final String title;
  final String value;
  final IconData icon;
  final Color color;

  const _SummaryCard({
    required this.title,
    required this.value,
    required this.icon,
    required this.color,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return ArchCard(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(ArchSpacing.lg),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: color.withOpacity(0.1),
              borderRadius: ArchBorders.medium,
            ),
            child: Icon(icon, size: 20, color: color),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.md),
          Text(title, style: ArchTextStyles.bodySmall),
          const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.xs),
          Text(value, style: ArchTextStyles.h3),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _InvoiceCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final Invoice invoice;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  const _InvoiceCard({
    required this.invoice,
    required this.onTap,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: ArchSpacing.md),
      child: ArchCard(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(ArchSpacing.lg),
        onTap: onTap,
        child: Row(
          children: [
            // Status Icon
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: _getStatusColor(invoice.status).withOpacity(0.1),
                shape: BoxShape.circle,
              ),
              child: Icon(
                _getStatusIcon(invoice.status),
                size: 20,
                color: _getStatusColor(invoice.status),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: ArchSpacing.lg),
            
            // Invoice Info
            Expanded(
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Text('Fatura #${invoice.number}', 
                    style: ArchTextStyles.body.copyWith(
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.xs),
                  Text(
                    DateFormat('dd/MM/yyyy').format(invoice.dueDate),
                    style: ArchTextStyles.bodySmall.copyWith(
                      color: ArchColors.textSecondary,
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            
            // Amount and Status
            Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
              children: [
                Text(
                  'R\$${invoice.amount.toStringAsFixed(2)}',
                  style: ArchTextStyles.h3.copyWith(fontSize: 18),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.xs),
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: _getStatusColor(invoice.status).withOpacity(0.1),
                    borderRadius: ArchBorders.small,
                  ),
                  child: Text(
                    _getStatusText(invoice.status),
                    style: ArchTextStyles.caption.copyWith(
                      color: _getStatusColor(invoice.status),
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Color _getStatusColor(InvoiceStatus status) {
    switch (status) {
      case InvoiceStatus.paid:
        return ArchColors.success;
      case InvoiceStatus.pending:
        return ArchColors.warning;
      case InvoiceStatus.overdue:
        return ArchColors.error;
    }
  }

  IconData _getStatusIcon(InvoiceStatus status) {
    switch (status) {
      case InvoiceStatus.paid:
        return Icons.check_circle_rounded;
      case InvoiceStatus.pending:
        return Icons.pending_rounded;
      case InvoiceStatus.overdue:
        return Icons.warning_rounded;
    }
  }

  String _getStatusText(InvoiceStatus status) {
    switch (status) {
      case InvoiceStatus.paid:
        return 'PAGO';
      case InvoiceStatus.pending:
        return 'PENDENTE';
      case InvoiceStatus.overdue:
        return 'ATRASADO';
    }
  }
}

7. SISTEMA DE DIAGNÓSTICO EM TEMPO REAL
dart
// ARQUIVO: lib/ui/diagnostics/arch_diagnostics_screen.dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../core/arch_theme.dart';
import '../../core/arch_components.dart';

class ArchDiagnosticsScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final String cpfCnpj;
  final String clientName;

  const ArchDiagnosticsScreen({
    super.key,
    required this.cpfCnpj,
    required this.clientName,
  });

  @override
  State<ArchDiagnosticsScreen> createState() => _ArchDiagnosticsScreenState();
}

class _ArchDiagnosticsScreenState extends State<ArchDiagnosticsScreen> 
    with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  
  late AnimationController _animationController;
  bool _isRunning = false;
  double _progress = 0.0;
  List<DiagnosticStep> _steps = [];

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _animationController = AnimationController(
      vsync: this,
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 1000),
    );
    _initializeSteps();
  }

  void _initializeSteps() {
    _steps = [
      DiagnosticStep(
        title: 'Verificar Dispositivo',
        description: 'Analisando hardware e sistema',
        status: StepStatus.pending,
        icon: Icons.phone_iphone_rounded,
      ),
      DiagnosticStep(
        title: 'Testar Conexão Wi-Fi',
        description: 'Verificando força do sinal',
        status: StepStatus.pending,
        icon: Icons.wifi_rounded,
      ),
      DiagnosticStep(
        title: 'Ping para Roteador',
        description: 'Testando latência local',
        status: StepStatus.pending,
        icon: Icons.router_rounded,
      ),
      DiagnosticStep(
        title: 'Teste de Velocidade',
        description: 'Medindo download e upload',
        status: StepStatus.pending,
        icon: Icons.speed_rounded,
      ),
      DiagnosticStep(
        title: 'Conexão com Internet',
        description: 'Verificando DNS e gateway',
        status: StepStatus.pending,
        icon: Icons.public_rounded,
      ),
    ];
  }

  void _startDiagnostics() async {
    setState(() {
      _isRunning = true;
      _progress = 0.0;
    });

    for (int i = 0; i < _steps.length; i++) {
      await _runStep(i);
      setState(() {
        _progress = (i + 1) / _steps.length;
      });
    }

    setState(() => _isRunning = false);
  }

  Future<void> _runStep(int index) async {
    setState(() => _steps[index].status = StepStatus.running);
    
    // Simulate step execution
    await Future.delayed(const Duration(seconds: 2));
    
    // Simulate random success/failure
    final success = index != 2; // Step 3 will fail for demo
    setState(() {
      _steps[index].status = success ? StepStatus.completed : StepStatus.failed;
      _steps[index].result = success ? 'Concluído com sucesso' : 'Falha na conexão';
    });
  }

  void _stopDiagnostics() {
    setState(() {
      _isRunning = false;
      _progress = 0.0;
      for (var step in _steps) {
        if (step.status == StepStatus.running) {
          step.status = StepStatus.pending;
        }
      }
    });
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _animationController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: ArchColors.background,
      appBar: ArchAppBar(
        title: 'Diagnóstico de Rede',
        showBackButton: true,
      ),
      body: Column(
        children: [
          // Progress Section
          _buildProgressSection(),
          const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.lg),
          
          // Steps List
          Expanded(
            child: ListView.builder(
              physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(),
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: ArchSpacing.md),
              itemCount: _steps.length,
              itemBuilder: (context, index) => _DiagnosticStepCard(
                step: _steps[index],
                index: index,
              ),
            ),
          ),
          
          // Action Button
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(ArchSpacing.md),
            child: _isRunning
                ? ArchButton(
                    text: 'Parar Diagnóstico',
                    onPressed: _stopDiagnostics,
                    filled: false,
                    icon: Icons.stop_circle_rounded,
                  )
                : ArchButton(
                    text: 'Iniciar Diagnóstico',
                    onPressed: _startDiagnostics,
                    icon: Icons.play_arrow_rounded,
                  ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildProgressSection() {
    return ArchCard(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.all(ArchSpacing.md),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(ArchSpacing.lg),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          // Progress Header
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Text(
                _isRunning ? 'Diagnóstico em Andamento' : 'Pronto para Diagnosticar',
                style: ArchTextStyles.h3,
              ),
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 6),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: _isRunning 
                      ? ArchColors.warning.withOpacity(0.1)
                      : ArchColors.success.withOpacity(0.1),
                  borderRadius: ArchBorders.small,
                ),
                child: Text(
                  _isRunning ? 'EXECUTANDO' : 'PRONTO',
                  style: ArchTextStyles.caption.copyWith(
                    color: _isRunning ? ArchColors.warning : ArchColors.success,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.lg),
          
          // Progress Bar
          LinearProgressIndicator(
            value: _progress,
            backgroundColor: ArchColors.textTertiary.withOpacity(0.2),
            valueColor: AlwaysStoppedAnimation<Color>(ArchColors.primary),
            borderRadius: ArchBorders.circle,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.md),
          
          // Progress Text
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Text(
                'Progresso',
                style: ArchTextStyles.bodySmall,
              ),
              Text(
                '${(_progress * 100).toStringAsFixed(0)}%',
                style: ArchTextStyles.body.copyWith(
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _DiagnosticStepCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final DiagnosticStep step;
  final int index;

  const _DiagnosticStepCard({
    required this.step,
    required this.index,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: ArchSpacing.md),
      child: ArchCard(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(ArchSpacing.lg),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            // Step Number and Status
            Container(
              width: 40,
              height: 40,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: _getStatusColor(step.status).withOpacity(0.1),
                shape: BoxShape.circle,
                border: Border.all(
                  color: _getStatusColor(step.status).withOpacity(0.3),
                ),
              ),
              child: Center(
                child: _getStatusIcon(step.status, index + 1),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: ArchSpacing.lg),
            
            // Step Info
            Expanded(
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Text(step.title, style: ArchTextStyles.body.copyWith(
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                  )),
                  const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.xs),
                  Text(step.description, 
                    style: ArchTextStyles.bodySmall.copyWith(
                      color: ArchColors.textSecondary,
                    ),
                  ),
                  if (step.result != null) ...[
                    const SizedBox(height: ArchSpacing.sm),
                    Text(step.result!,
                      style: ArchTextStyles.caption.copyWith(
                        color: _getStatusColor(step.status),
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Color _getStatusColor(StepStatus status) {
    switch (status) {
      case StepStatus.pending:
        return ArchColors.textTertiary;
      case StepStatus.running:
        return ArchColors.warning;
      case StepStatus.completed:
        return ArchColors.success;
      case StepStatus.failed:
        return ArchColors.error;
    }
  }

  Widget _getStatusIcon(StepStatus status, int stepNumber) {
    switch (status) {
      case StepStatus.pending:
        return Text(
          stepNumber.toString(),
          style: ArchTextStyles.caption.copyWith(
            color: ArchColors.textTertiary,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
          ),
        );
      case StepStatus.running:
        return SizedBox(
          width: 16,
          height: 16,
          child: CircularProgressIndicator(
            strokeWidth: 2,
            valueColor: AlwaysStoppedAnimation<Color>(ArchColors.warning),
          ),
        );
      case StepStatus.completed:
        return Icon(Icons.check_rounded, 
            size: 16, color: ArchColors.success);
      case StepStatus.failed:
        return Icon(Icons.close_rounded, 
            size: 16, color: ArchColors.error);
    }
  }
}

// Data Models for Diagnostics
enum StepStatus { pending, running, completed, failed }

class DiagnosticStep {
  final String title;
  final String description;
  StepStatus status;
  String? result;
  final IconData icon;

  DiagnosticStep({
    required this.title,
    required this.description,
    required this.status,
    this.result,
    required this.icon,
  });
}
